# Baseline profile (S169, 2026-09-02). Perf-only: tells ART which methods to AOT-compile at
# install instead of interpreting them until the first background dexopt (measured on
# SM-S901W: interpreted keystroke frames p50 24 ms / p95 63-90 ms vs compiled p50 ~9 ms).
#
# 1) OUR code, exhaustively — the engine's lattice/fuzzy/compound/negation layers and the
#    keyboard's touch/strip/glide paths must be compiled no matter which words a session
#    happened to type. Wildcards are expanded by AGP against the final (R8) dex.
HSPLcom/banglu/engine/**->**(**)**
HSPLcom/banglu/keyboard/**->**(**)**
Lcom/banglu/engine/**
Lcom/banglu/keyboard/**
#
# 2) Framework/library hot set observed by ART during a realistic session on the
#    unobfuscated release build (cold start, Bangla+English typing, chips, backspace, glide,
#    symbols, emoji, clipboard). Regenerate with:
#    adb shell cmd package dump-profiles --dump-classes-and-methods com.banglu.keyboard
#    (only from a -dontobfuscate build; an obfuscated build exports R8 names).
